What if use multiple reversed terms with delays to spread cross peaks? Maybe choose a random mixture of shifts of central, so that each component's flat region covers different part of [0,2n]. Since components nonnegative, sum may cancel? No cancel, but if shifts are many, peaks distribute. This suggests a "randomized shift" construction! Let base central sequence C length m (supports about m, square flat over m lags but support 2m). If we take a sequence as a sum of shifted copies of a shorter base, with shifts separated by m, the self-convolutions may add. But if shifts are spread over total length n, each base copy contributes a flat plateau over some range; sum of plateaus may be smoother, maybe average. However nonnegative sums add; to make P^2 flat over 0..2n, we want each sum of two positions. Another viewpoint: A sequence p whose support is union of intervals with certain densities. If we take a random convex combination of shifted one-sided heavy-tailed distributions, the sum distribution is convolution of the distribution of base X and shift. If shifts are random with some distribution, sum distribution is convolution of base sum distribution (flat over base support) with shift distribution. This can extend flatness! Let's formalize.

Let base B of length L with B*B approximately flat over [0,L] and decays after? If we take random shift S from a distribution D over [0,n-L] and define P(z)=E[ z^S B(z) ] (i.e. average over shifted copies), then P is the convolution of D and B? In coefficient domain, P = D*B (linear convolution). Then P^2 = (D*B)^2 = (D*D)*(B*B). If B*B is flat over [0,L] and decays, convolving with D*D may smooth/extend. If D is uniform over long interval, D*D triangular, not flat; but if D itself has convolution flat? We can choose D as central distribution (one-sided) to make D*D flat over its range. Then P^2 is convolution of two flat kernels, which may become triangular, not flat. Hmm.

Alternative: P = average of shifted central sequences with random shifts. Then P^2 = ∑_{s,t} D_s D_t z^{s+t} C^2. If C^2 is flat (approx) over [0,2L], the coefficient at k is sum over s,t with k in [s+t, s+t+2L] D_s D_t. If D is many points, for interior k with many shifts, the indicator overlaps; if D_s uniform density, integral over (s,t) such that |k-(s+t)|≤L. For continuous D density d(x) on [0,N], P^2_k ≈ ∫∫ d(s)d(t) 1_{|k-s-t|≤L}. If d is constant, this count grows with k (triangular) then plateau then decreases—triangular, not flat. But if d has singular endpoints, maybe convolution with box yields flat. We want choose d such that convolution of (d*d) with flat box produces flat. Since C^2 flat over long window, it effectively computes moving average of D_D over lag k. We need moving average of D*D over window L constant. The simplest D*D is itself flat over larger range; then moving average flat. D can be one-sided heavy-tailed (central) whose square flat over [0,N]. So hierarchically: choose D of length R (or N) optimized; convolve its square with box (C^2) to produce approximate flat if flatness scales. This is same multilevel but now convolution in coefficient with box, not product? P = D * C (ordinary convolution) length n=N+L. P^2 = D^2 * C^2. If C^2 is boxcar (flat) and D^2 is flat over N, then P^2 is convolution of two flat sequences: if lengths compatible, maybe flat over N+L? Actually convolution of two constant sequences of lengths A and B yields triangular length A+B-1 with plateau if one? If both constant, convolution is trapezoid: increases then constant if lengths differ, then decreases. Max at plateau = min(A,B)*const. Sum lengths etc. Score? ```

Let's design functions in detail:

```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys
try: from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except: minimize=None, linprog=None
```

Constants: `UB_FINAL=1000.0`, maybe.

`clean_seq(a)`:
- np.asarray float, ravel, finite, nan to 0, clip [0,UB_FINAL], if sum<0.01 return zeros? If size 0 random central.

`exact_score(a)`:
- clean, s=sum; if s<0.01 inf; c=np.convolve(a,a); m=max(c.real?) c float; if not finite inf; val=2*n*m/(s*s).
Use `np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)`. For large n, np.convolve O(n^2) but n<=2048 okay.

`fft_conv(a, out_len=None)`:
```
n=a.size; l=1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length(); f=np.fft.rfft(a,l); q=np.fft.irfft(f*f,l)[:2*n-1]; return q.real
```
Clip small negative for weights. If n=0 return.

`fast_score(a)`:
```
a=clean a; s=sum; if s<0.01 inf; q=fft_conv(a); m=float(np.max(q if q.size else [0])); if m<0 and small:0; val=2*n*m/(s*s); return val
```

`smooth_value_gradP(a, beta)`:

`product_obj_state(xc,xw?)` with state length m+r:
- split xc=state[:m], xw=state[m:]

- val=2*n*L
- gp shape (r,m). Gw = (gp * c[None,:]).sum(axis=1); Gc = (gp * w[:,None]).sum(axis=0)
- gradw = w*(Gw - dot(w,Gw)); gradc = c*(Gc - dot(c,Gc))
- return val, np.concatenate([gradc, gradw]) depending state split. We'll state = [xc, xw]; return grad state.
```
if nonfinite: return 1e100, zeros.
```

def lp_move(p, max_iters, time_per_lp):
  p=clean normalized sum1
  best=p; best_score=exact_score(p)
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    q=fft_convolution(p); M=max; if not finite break
    # solve with T = M (or factor)
    factors = [1.0,1.1,0.9] maybe loop
    for factor in factors:
       T=M*factor
       A=build toeplitz p
       res=linprog(...)
       if res.ok: g=...; line search; if improved break factors
    if no break: break
  return best, best_score
```

`build_lp_matrix(p)` top-level:
```
n=p.size; rows=2*n-1; A=lil(rows,n)
for k in range(rows):
  start=max(0,k-(n-1)); end=min(n-1,k)
  if start<=end:
    A[k,start:end+1]=p[k-start:k-end+1]
return A.tocsr()
```

Implementation of `smooth_L_gradP`:
```
def smooth_L_gradP(a,beta):
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or beta<=0: return inf zeros
    q=fft_convolution(a)
    if q.size==0: return inf zeros
    q=np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
    C=float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(C) or C<=0:
       return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
    z=beta*(q-C)
    z=np.clip(z,-745.0,0.0)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    great=float(np.sum(ez))
    if great<=0 or not finite: ...
    L=C + math.log(great)/beta
    w=ez/great
    lg=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    f=np.fft.rfft(a, lg)
    g=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], lg)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(f*g, lg)
    gradP=2.0*conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
    gradP=np.nan_to_num(...)
    if not finite: return inf zeros
    return L, gradP
```
For n=1, `2*n-2 - np.arange(1) =0`, lg=next_pow2(1)=1, rfft length1, wrev length1, conv length1, grad=2*conv[0]? P=[p], q=[p²], weights1, conv=p*p? rec array P length1, wrev length1, conv[0]=p*1 = p? Wait wrev is weights not multiplied by P? I set g=rfft(wrev); f=rfft(a). conv = a * wrev. For n=1, conv[0]=p*1 =p. gradP expected 2*p? Yes gradP=2conv =2p. Correct.
For n=2 earlier conv length4, wrev length3, lg next_pow2(4)? 3n-2=4, next_pow2(4)=4. rfft length4, conv length4. Good.

But `np.fft.irfft` returns length lg, circular convolution? We need linear convolution of lengths n and 2n-1, length 3n-2. Using zero padding to lg >= 3n-2 gives linear conv. Good.

For `fft_convolution(a)` linear length 2n-1 with lg>=2n-1. Good.

`fast_score` uses `fft_convolution`, q max. Since a sum1, q can be small. Use exact if n<=64 maybe? Not needed.

`exact_score`: For n large, `np.convolve` direct could be slow. For final exact maybe 2048 direct ~4M, fine. In LP line search, maybe exact 1024 9 times. Good. Could use fast for speed and exact for final; but to avoid discrepancy due rounding, exact for best.

Detailed functions:

```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    p=1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p
```
Loop small; for large m okay. Or bit_length. Use bit_length for speed:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << ((m-1).bit_length())
```
For m=1: (0).bit_length()=0 ->1. Good.

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    n=int(a.size)
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    l=next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(a,l)
    q=np.fft.irfft(f*f,l)
    return q[:2*n-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
Use `2*n-1` not 2n-2; length enough. For n=1, l=1.

`smooth_L_gradP`: `conv_len_needed = n + (2*n-1) -1 = 3*n-2`; next_pow2. If n=1, 1*1? 3*1-2=1. good.

`full_obj_state`: Need return tuple (fun, grad) for scipy. If x size mismatch? state fixed.
```
def full_obj_state(state,n,beta):
   x=np.asarray(state, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if x.size != n: return 1e100, np.zeros(x.size)
   x=x-np.mean(x)
   p=softmax(x)
   L,gp=smooth_L_gradP(p,beta)
   if not math.isfinite(L): return 1e100, zeros
   val=2.0*n*L
   gpv=2.0*n*gp
   avg=float(np.dot(p,gpv))
   grad=p*(gpv-avg)
   grad=np.nan_to_num(...)
   if not finite: ...
   return float(val), grad
```
`np.dot(p,gpv)` scalar. Since grad sum zero. Good.

`product_obj_state`:
```
def product_obj_state(state,m,r,n,beta):
  x=... size m+r. xc=x[:m]; xw=x[m:]
  xc=xc-np.mean(xc); xw=xw-np.mean(xw)
  c=softmax(xc); w=softmax(xw)
  P=(w[:,None]*c[None,:]).reshape(-1)
  L,gp=smooth_L_gradP(P,beta)
  if not finite: return ...
  factor=2.0*n
  Gp=factor*gp.reshape(r,m)
  Gw=(Gp*c[None,:]).sum(axis=1)
  Gc=(Gp*w[:,None]).sum(axis=0)
  avgw=float(np.dot(w,Gw)); avgc=float(np.dot(c,Gc))
  gradw=w*(Gw-avgw)
  gradc=c*(Gc-avgc)
  grad=np.concatenate((gradc,gradw))
  val=factor*L
  if not finite: ...
  return val,grad
```
If P length r*m maybe not n if n mismatch; in smooth uses n=P.size not passed? We pass n to factor but P length r*m. Should use `nprod=P.size` for factor? In product_obj, `n` should equal r*m. If not, use `nP = r*m`. The score formula length is actual P length. Use `nprod = P.size`; factor=2*nprod. But global `_G_PROD_N` can be set to r*m. Good.

def lp_move(p, time_limit, it_limit=3):
  best=p; best_score=exact_score(p); deadline=now+time_limit
  for it in range(it_limit):
    if now>=deadline-10: break
    if p.size>1024: break
    T=factor*max(q)
    A=build
    try res=linprog(...)
    except: break
    if not success: break
    g=clip; gs=sum; if gs<0: break
    # line search
    improved=False
    for t in ts:
      mixed=(1-t)*p + t*(g/gs)
      sc=exact_score(mixed)
      if sc<best_score: update, improved=True
    if not improved:
      # try T=1.1M? maybe loop factor in outer
      break
  return best,best_score
```

`anneal_fast`: Use fast_score and normalize. At end exact.
```
def anneal_fast(a,budget,rng):
  a=clean; s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s
  cur=a.copy(); cur_score=fast_score(cur); best=a.copy(); best_score=exact_score(best)
  sigma=0.1; T=0.005; no=0; deadline=...
  while now<deadline:
    cand=make_noisy_candidate(cur,rng,sigma)
    cs=fast_score(cand)
    if finite:
      accept = cs <= cur_score or (rng.random() < exp(-(cs-cur_score)/T))
      if accept: cur=cand; cur_score=cs; if cs<best_score: best=cand.copy(); best_score=cs; no=0 else no+=1
      else no+=1
    if no>=80: cur=best; cur_score=fast_score(cur); no=0; sigma=max(0.02,0.7*sigma); T=max(1e-4,0.7*T)
    if step%50==0: sigma*=0.98; T*=0.99
  # final exact best and maybe cand exact if fast approximate? exact_score(best)
  return best, exact_score(best)
```
`make_noisy_candidate` ensure normalize:
```
a=np.asarray; n=a.size; nblocks=int(rng.integers(1,4)); maxw=max(1, min(64,n//8)); width=int(rng.integers(1,max(2,maxw+1))); start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1)); scale=np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,width)); a[start:start+width]*=scale
if rng.random()<0.2: a=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
return a

def run_minimize_fun(obj, x0, bounds, maxiter, maxfun):
  if minimize is None: return x0
  try:
    res=minimize(fun=obj, x0=x0, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options={...})
    x=res.x
  except: x=x0
  return x
```

Now, check product L-BFGS objective flat directions and bounds. Since x centered in function, if L-BFGS moves x along a constant vector, value same but gradient? Let's derive gradient with centered x: We compute p=softmax(x-mean). The derivative of p wrt x includes centering, but our gradient transform `p*(G - avg)` already assumes p=softmax(x) without centering? For softmax with any shift, p same, and gradient wrt x (uncentered) sums zero. If we internally center x before softmax, the mapping x->p is invariant to shift, and gradient wrt x is still p*(G - p·G) because centering derivative is rank one? Let's verify: Let y=x-mean(x). p=softmax(y). Variation δx = v + c (shift) where c is chosen? y variation =δx - mean(δx). ∂p/∂x_k = p*(e_k - p - (1/n)(1 - 1?))? The derivative of centered y: y_i = x_i - (1/n)∑x_j. δy_i = δ_{ik}δx_k - (1/n)∑δx_j. Then dF = ∑_i ∂F/∂p_i dp_i. ∂F/∂x_k = ∑_i G_i ∑_j (∂p_i/∂y_j)(δ_{jk} -1/n) δx_j? Linearize. For object flat under global shift, the resulting gradient should still be p*(G-avg) and sum zero. The ranking? It should be okay; scaling by factor maybe. Let's calculate: Without centering, grad_x = p*(G - p·G). With centering, y = x - mean; softmax(y)=softmax(x) exactly (shift-invariant). The derivative should be same because the function composition f(x-mean) has gradient f'(x-mean) * (I - 1/n 11^T). But f'(y) for softmax objective is grad_x_uncentered(y), which sums zero. Multiplying by projection doesn't change it. So same. Good.

Let's write a bit of code mentally for `central_seq`:
```
def central_seq(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1): a[i+1]=a[i]*(i+0.5)/(i+1.0)
    return a
```
For n large, compute iterative. Good.

`build_lp_matrix` uses sparse. If not, returns None.
```
def build_lp_matrix(p):
  if sparse is None: return None
  n=p.size; rows=2*n-1; A=sparse.lil_matrix((rows,n), dtype=np.float64)
  for k in range(rows):
    start=0 if k < n-1 else k-(n-1)
    end=n-1 if k > n-1 else k
    # simpler:
    start=max(0, k-(n-1)); end=min(n-1,k)
    if start <= end:
       vals = p[k-end : k-start+1][::-1]
       A[k, start:end+1] = vals
  return A.tocsr()
```
Check slice lengths: For k=5,n=5 start=1,end=4, k-end=1,k-start+1=5, p[1:5][::-1]=p4,p3,p2,p1 for j=1..4: p4,p3,p2,p1 correct. Good.

Anneal: At start normalize. If `budget_s` small, skip. In loop, `step_count=0; deadline_local=...`.
Print every 200 steps maybe. Use exact? `best_score` exact. `cur_score` fast. When update best, use `sc=fast_score` but maybe approximate; we can occasionally exact? At end exact. If fast false improvement, final exact still correct-ish. For best, we can also evaluate exact every 50 steps? Too expensive. Use fast. But to avoid returning a sequence whose exact worse due FFT error, at end exact_score(best) and compare to previous best exact. Keep `global_best_exact`? In anneal, we pass current best_a,best_score exact. At end, if exact_score(best_candidate) < best_score exact update. That's enough. During anneal best based fast; at end candidate best may have exact not less, then return original. Good.

Let's write final code block. Need ensure code length manageable.

Review for no lambdas: In `sort_first` top-level. No lambda.

Potential bug: `np.fft.irfft(f * f, l)`: `f*f` elementwise, okay. For real a.

Potential bug: In `smooth_L_gradP`, if `beta` very large and q-C negative for all but max, ez sum at least 1; L=C. Good.

Potential bug: In `product_obj_state`, `gp` length may be P.size. If P sum 1 but some values extremely small, grad finite.

Potential bug: In `full_obj_state`, `gpv = factor * gp`, then `avg = float(np.dot(p, gpv))`. Since objective f=2nL, and L homogeneous degree? L is not homogeneous degree? q degree2; L approximates max q, degree2. factor okay. For probabilities sum1, val=2nL. Good.

Potential bug: In `smooth_L_gradP`, gradient of L wrt P: If L=C + log(sumexp)/beta, derivative wrt q_k=w_k. Then gradP=2 diag? We use convolution with weights. Formula gradP_i = 2 ∑_{j} P_j w_{i+j}. We computed conv with w[::-1]. Test n=2 earlier. Good.

Let's re-evaluate formula with P length n, w length 2n-1. `conv = np.convolve(P, w[::-1])` length 3n-2. Index `2n-2 - i`. For i=0, conv[2n-2] = sum_j P_j w_rev[2n-2-j] = P_j w[j]? w_rev[t]=w[q_len-1 - t]=w[2n-2 - t]. Set t=2n-2-j => w[j]. Sum j 0..n-1 if t<n? For j≤n-1, t≥n-1; w_rev index valid up to 2n-3? t for j=0:2n-2, valid; for j=n-1: n-1, valid. Good gradP0=2∑ P_j w_j. For i=1, conv[2n-3] = ∑ P_j w_rev[2n-3-j]=∑P_j w[1+j]. Good. So formula okay.

Gradient scaled by 2. For q=P*P, derivative c_k = ∑_i P_i P_{k-i}; d c_k/dP_i =2 P_{k-i} if valid. Then g_i =∑_k w_k 2P_{k-i} =2∑_j P_j w_{i+j}. yes.

Now, product gradient Gw: dP_{i,j}/dw_i = c_j (not 2) because P = w_i c_j. But L gradient wrt P is dL/dP_{i,j}. So dL/dw_i =∑_j dL/dP_{i,j} * c_j. Then factor 2n. Good.

Now, in full gradient, P_i = p_i, dP_i/dp_i=1. Then simple.

Now, if softmax state centering: Gradient transform should include factor. We do `gpv = factor*gp`, `avg=dot(p,gpv)`, grad=p*(gpv-avg). This is derivative of val = factor L wrt state x. Since dP_i/dx_k = p_i(δik-p_k). Good.

Now, product gradient: `Gp = factor*gp.reshape(r,m)`. The derivative of val wrt w_i before softmax =∑_j Gp_{i,j} c_j = Gw_i. Then derivative wrt xw_k = w_k(Gw_k - ∑ w_i Gw_i). Yes. For c similarly. Good.

Now, after L-BFGS, state raw may have large mean; `product_state_to_p` centers before softmax. If xbest has mean large, okay. But if xbest raw outside bounds? We clip. Good.

Now, exact_score of p normalized sum1: val=2n*qmax. If qmax maybe 0, val0. Good.

Let's consider if product objective is flat along scaling parts? Softsum c sum1, w sum1, no scale flat beyond one direction each? There are two flat directions: adding constant to xc or xw. Gradient zero. L-BFGS-B bounded. Fine.

```python
"""
Non-negative convolution peak-factor optimizer.

This program searches for a nonnegative sequence a minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
within a 400-second budget.

The method combines several complementary ideas:
1. Fast exact-compatible scoring via FFT, with exact np.convolve checks for saved
   best candidates.
2. Low-dimensional product / two-scale constructions
       P(z) = C(z) W(z^m),
   where P is nonnegative and sum(P)=1.  These families can make the square
   polynomial P(z)^2 much flatter than a plain one-sided central sequence.
3. Full softmax parameterization of a probability sequence optimized by
   L-BFGS-B on a log-sum-exp smoothing of the peak.
4. A sparse LP linearization move inspired by the referenced state-of-the-art.
5. Final block-perturbation annealing using fast FFT scoring.

The function propose_candidate() is the required entry point.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
    from scipy import sparse
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None
    sparse = None

TARGET_UPPER_BOUND = 1.5027
UB_FINAL = 1000.0

# Global scratch variables used by the top-level objective wrappers.
_G_FULL_N = 1
_G_FULL_BETA = 1.0

_G_PROD_M = 1
_G_PROD_R = 1
_G_PROD_N = 1
_G_PROD_BETA = 1.0


def central_seq(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (i + 0.5) / float(i + 1)
    return a


def sanitize_sequence(a):
    """Convert to a 1D float array, replacing nonfinite/out-of-range values."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=UB_FINAL, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, UB_FINAL)
    return arr


def make_valid_sequence(a, fallback_n=128):
    """Return a valid nonnegative sequence, using a central fallback if needed."""
    arr = sanitize_sequence(a)
    if arr.size == 0 or float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        arr = central_seq(fallback_n).astype(np.float64)
    if float(np.sum(arr)) < 0.01:
        arr = central_seq(128).astype(np.float64)
    return np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact evaluator-compatible score, using np.convolve."""
    arr = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        c = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << ((m - 1).bit_length())


def fft_convolution(a):
    """Fast linear convolution a*a, length 2*n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    q = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)
    return np.asarray(q[:2 * n - 1], dtype=np.float64)


def fast_score(a):
    """FFT approximation to exact_score, fast for repeated calls."""
    arr = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        q = fft_convolution(arr)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if q.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        if m < 0.0:
            m = 0.0
    val = float(2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s))
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(x, -745.0, 745.0))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(x, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def full_state_to_p(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    p = softmax(x)
    return p


def product_state_to_p(x, m, r):
    m = int(m)
    r = int(r)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m + r:
        x = np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)
    xc = x[:m].copy()
    xw = x[m:].copy()
    xc = xc - float(np.mean(xc))
    xw = xw - float(np.mean(xw))
    c = softmax(xc)
    w = softmax(xw)
    p = (w[:, None] * c[None, :]).reshape(-1)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
        p = p / s
    return p


def smooth_L_gradP(a, beta):
    """
    Return (L, gradL) where L ~= max(a*a) by log-sum-exp.
    gradL is dL/da.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(beta):
        beta = 1.0
    beta = float(min(max(1.0, beta), 1.0e7))

    try:
        q = fft_convolution(a)
    except Exception:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if q.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    C = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(C) or C <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = beta * (q - C)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    great = float(np.sum(ez))
    if great <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(great):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = C + math.log(great) / beta
    wq = ez / great

    # grad_i(L) = 2 * sum_j a_j wq_{i+j}
    need = max(1, 3 * n - 2)
    Lg = next_pow2(need)
    try:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Lg)
        fw = np.fft.rfft(wq[::-1], Lg)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, Lg)
        gradP = 2.0 * conv[2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)]
    except Exception:
        # Fallback direct O(n^2), only used as guard.
        gradP = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            hi = min(2 * n - 2, i + n - 1)
            lo = i
            if lo <= hi:
                gradP[i] = 2.0 * float(np.dot(a, wq[lo:hi + 1]))

    gradP = np.nan_to_num(gradP, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gradP)) or not math.isfinite(L):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(L), gradP.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def full_obj_state(state, n, beta):
    x = np.asarray(state, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0, n), dtype=np.float64)

    p = full_state_to_p(x, n)
    L, gp = smooth_L_gradP(p, beta)
    if not math.isfinite(L):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    factor = 2.0 * float(n)
    val = factor * L
    g = factor * gp
    avg = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad = p * (g - avg)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)) or not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def product_obj_state(state, m, r, beta):
    m = int(m)
    r = int(r)
    x = np.asarray(state, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != m + r:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)

    xc = x[:m].copy()
    xw = x[m:].copy()
    xc = xc - float(np.mean(xc))
    xw = xw - float(np.mean(xw))
    c = softmax(xc)
    w = softmax(xw)
    P = (w[:, None] * c[None, :]).reshape(-1)
    nP = int(P.size)
    if nP <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)

    L, gp = smooth_L_gradP(P, beta)
    if not math.isfinite(L):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)

    factor = 2.0 * float(nP)
    val = factor * L
    Gp = factor * gp.reshape(r, m)

    Gw = (Gp * c[None, :]).sum(axis=1)
    Gc = (Gp * w[:, None]).sum(axis=0)

    avgw = float(np.dot(w, Gw))
    avgc = float(np.dot(c, Gc))
    gradw = w * (Gw - avgw)
    gradc = c * (Gc - avgc)

    grad = np.concatenate((gradc, gradw)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)) or not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad


def _full_obj(state):
    return full_obj_state(state, _G_FULL_N, _G_FULL_BETA)


def _prod_obj(state):
    return product_obj_state(state, _G_PROD_M, _G_PROD_R, _G_PROD_BETA)


def run_minimize_fun(obj, x0, bounds, maxiter, maxfun):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if minimize is None or x0.size == 0:
        return x0
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun=obj,
            x0=x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(max(1, maxiter)),
                "maxfun": int(max(10, maxfun)),
                "maxls": 50,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        xx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        xx = x0
    if xx.size != x0.size:
        xx = x0
    xx = np.nan_to_num(xx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xx = np.clip(xx, -50.0, 50.0)
    return xx


def optimize_full_start(p0, time_limit):
    """Optimize a full length-n sequence from an initial probability p0."""
    p0 = sanitize_sequence(p0)
    n = int(p0.size)
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return p0, exact_score(p0)

    s = float(np.sum(p0))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        p0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p0 = p0 / s

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best_p)

    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))
    x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -40.0, 40.0)
    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * n

    try:
        qmax = float(np.max(fft_convolution(p0)))
    except Exception:
        qmax = 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        qmax = 1.0 / float(max(1, n))

    beta1 = float(min(1.0e6, max(1.0, 10.0 / max(qmax, 1e-12))))
    beta2 = float(min(1.0e6, max(beta1, 50.0 / max(qmax, 1e-12))))

    if n <= 128:
        mi1, mi2 = 300, 150
    elif n <= 384:
        mi1, mi2 = 200, 100
    elif n <= 1200:
        mi1, mi2 = 130, 70
    else:
        mi1, mi2 = 80, 40

    mf = int(max(1500, 25 * max(mi1, mi2)))

    global _G_FULL_N, _G_FULL_BETA
    _G_FULL_N = n

    now = time.monotonic()
    deadline = now + max(0.0, time_limit - 0.5)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline and minimize is not None:
        _G_FULL_BETA = beta1
        x1 = run_minimize_fun(_full_obj, x0, bounds, mi1, mf)
        p1 = full_state_to_p(x1, n)
        sc1 = exact_score(p1)
        if math.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_score = sc1
            x0 = x1.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0 and minimize is not None:
        # Re-center x0 before second phase.
        x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        x0 = np.nan_to_num(x0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
        _G_FULL_BETA = beta2
        x2 = run_minimize_fun(_full_obj, x0, bounds, mi2, mf)
        p2 = full_state_to_p(x2, n)
        sc2 = exact_score(p2)
        if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_score = sc2

    # Symmetry average can help rounding / expose nearby basins.
    sym = 0.5 * (best_p + best_p[::-1])
    scsym = exact_score(sym)
    if math.isfinite(scsym) and scsym < best_score - 1e-14:
        best_p = sym.copy()
        best_score = scsym

    best_p = make_valid_sequence(best_p, fallback_n=max(16, n))
    final_score = exact_score(best_p)
    if not math.isfinite(final_score) or final_score >= best_score:
        best_p = p0.copy()
        final_score = best_score
    return best_p, final_score


def make_component(n, spec, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    spec = str(spec)
    if spec == "central":
        v = central_seq(n).astype(np.float64).copy()
    elif spec == "rev":
        v = central_seq(n)[::-1].astype(np.float64).copy()
    elif spec == "uniform":
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif spec == "rand":
        v = 0.5 + 0.75 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif spec == "rand_decay":
        v = 0.5 + 0.75 * np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))
        v *= np.exp(-np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n // 4)))
    else:
        v = central_seq(n).astype(np.float64).copy()

    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=1.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 1e-300)

    if spec.startswith("noise") or rng.random() < 0.0:
        pass

    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    return v / s


def make_product_state(m, r, spec_c, spec_w, rng):
    m = int(m)
    r = int(r)
    c = make_component(m, spec_c, rng)
    w = make_component(r, spec_w, rng)
    xc = np.log(np.maximum(c, 1e-300))
    xw = np.log(np.maximum(w, 1e-300))
    xc = xc - float(np.mean(xc))
    xw = xw - float(np.mean(xw))
    x = np.concatenate((xc, xw)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    return x


def optimize_product_from_state(m, r, x0, time_limit):
    """Optimize the product family P(z)=C(z)W(z^m) from initial log state."""
    m = int(m)
    r = int(r)
    if m <= 0 or r <= 0 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x0.size != m + r:
        x0 = np.zeros(m + r, dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.nan_to_num(x0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -40.0, 40.0)

    p = product_state_to_p(x0, m, r)
    best_score = exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_x = x0.copy()

    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        return None, float("inf")

    global _G_PROD_M, _G_PROD_R, _G_PROD_N, _G_PROD_BETA
    _G_PROD_M = m
    _G_PROD_R = r
    _G_PROD_N = m * r

    try:
        qmax = float(np.max(fft_convolution(p)))
    except Exception:
        qmax = 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        qmax = 1.0 / float(max(1, m * r))

    beta1 = float(min(1.0e6, max(1.0, 10.0 / max(qmax, 1e-12))))
    beta2 = float(min(1.0e6, max(beta1, 50.0 / max(qmax, 1e-12))))

    d = m + r
    if d <= 40:
        mi1, mi2 = 220, 120
    elif d <= 100:
        mi1, mi2 = 150, 80
    elif d <= 220:
        mi1, mi2 = 100, 55
    else:
        mi1, mi2 = 60, 35

    maxfun = int(max(1200, 22 * max(mi1, mi2)))
    bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * d
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit - 0.5)

    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        _G_PROD_BETA = beta1
        x1 = run_minimize_fun(_prod_obj, best_x, bounds, mi1, maxfun)
        p1 = product_state_to_p(x1, m, r)
        sc1 = exact_score(p1)
        if math.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_score = sc1
            best_x = x1.copy()

    if minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        # Re-center.
        xb = best_x.copy()
        xb = xb - float(xb[:m].mean())
        xb[m:] = xb[m:] - float(xb[m:].mean())
        _G_PROD_BETA = beta2
        x2 = run_minimize_fun(_prod_obj, xb, bounds, mi2, maxfun)
        p2 = product_state_to_p(x2, m, r)
        sc2 = exact_score(p2)
        if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_score = sc2

    sym = 0.5 * (best_p + best_p[::-1])
    scsym = exact_score(sym)
    if math.isfinite(scsym) and scsym < best_score - 1e-14:
        best_p = sym.copy()
        best_score = scsym

    return best_p, best_score


def sort_by_first(item):
    return item[0]


def optimize_product_factor(n, m, r, budget, rng, current_best_score):
    """Try several initial product states for one (n,m,r)."""
    if m * r != n:
        return None, float("inf")
    if budget <= 5.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    high_pairs = [
        ("central", "central"),
        ("central", "uniform"),
        ("uniform", "central"),
        ("rev", "central"),
        ("central", "rev"),
    ]
    extra_pairs = [
        ("rand", "rand"),
        ("rand_decay", "central"),
        ("central", "rand_decay"),
        ("uniform", "uniform"),
    ]

    pairs = high_pairs[:] + extra_pairs
    start_budget = max(3.0, budget / float(max(1, len(high_pairs))))
    states = []
    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget

    # Generate and approximate-rank starts, but only as long as budget allows.
    for pair in pairs:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - start_budget:
            break
        try:
            st = make_product_state(m, r, pair[0], pair[1], rng)
            p = product_state_to_p(st, m, r)
            sc = fast_score(p)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            states.append((sc, st, p))

    if not states:
        return None, float("inf")

    states.sort(key=sort_by_first)

    best_p = None
    best_score = current_best_score

    # Only keep as many starts as time seems to allow.
    max_starts = 3
    if budget < 80.0:
        max_starts = 2
    if budget < 35.0:
        max_starts = 1

    for idx in range(min(max_starts, len(states))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 4.0:
            break
        sc0, st, p0 = states[idx]

        sc_init = exact_score(p0)
        if math.isfinite(sc_init) and sc_init < best_score:
            best_p = p0.copy()
            best_score = sc_init

        per_start_budget = min(deadline - time.monotonic(), 0.45 * budget)
        if per_start_budget < 4.0:
            continue
        p1, sc1 = optimize_product_from_state(m, r, st, per_start_budget)
        if p1 is not None and math.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_p = p1.copy()
            best_score = sc1

    return best_p, best_score


def optimize_product_factors(factors, best_a, best_score, budget, rng):
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_score = float(best_score)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget)

    for item in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            n, m, r = item
        except Exception:
            continue
        n = int(n)
        m = int(m)
        r = int(r)
        if n <= 0 or n > 4096 or m <= 0 or r <= 0 or m * r != n:
            continue

        remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
        per_factor = min(max(10.0, remain * 0.22), 70.0)
        if per_factor < 8.0:
            break

        p, sc = optimize_product_factor(n, m, r, per_factor, rng, best_score)
        if p is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            a = make_valid_sequence(p, fallback_n=n)
            sc2 = exact_score(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_score = sc2
                print(f"product n={n:5d} m={m:4d} r={r:4d} score={best_score:.10f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def optimize_full_set(candidates, best_a, best_score, budget, rng):
    """Run full softmax L-BFGS from a few promising candidate sequences."""
    if budget <= 10.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget)
    scored = []
    seen_sizes = set()

    for a in candidates:
        a = sanitize_sequence(a)
        if a.size <= 0 or a.size > 4096:
            continue
        if a.size in seen_sizes:
            continue
        try:
            sc = fast_score(a)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            s = float(np.sum(a))
            if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
                a = a / s
            scored.append((sc, a))
            seen_sizes.add(a.size)

    if not scored:
        return best_a, best_score

    scored.sort(key=sort_by_first)

    max_runs = 3
    if budget < 80.0:
        max_runs = 2
    if budget < 40.0:
        max_runs = 1

    for idx in range(min(max_runs, len(scored))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
            break
        sc0, a0 = scored[idx]

        # Do not waste time on a clearly worse candidate when best is already very good.
        if best_score <= TARGET_UPPER_BOUND and sc0 > best_score + 0.25:
            continue

        per_run = min(max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0), max(20.0, budget * 0.45))
        p1, sc1 = optimize_full_start(a0, per_run)
        if math.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            a1 = make_valid_sequence(p1, fallback_n=max(16, int(a0.size)))
            sc2 = exact_score(a1)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_a = a1.copy()
                best_score = sc2
                print(f"full polish n={a1.size:5d} score={best_score:.10f}")

    return best_a, best_score


def build_lp_matrix(p):
    if sparse is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    rows = 2 * n - 1
    A = sparse.lil_matrix((rows, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(rows):
        start = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        end = min(n - 1, k)
        if start <= end:
            vals = p[k - end : k - start + 1][::-1]
            A[k, start:end + 1] = vals
    try:
        return A.tocsr()
    except Exception:
        return None


def lp_move(p, time_limit, max_iter=3):
    """SOTA-inspired sparse LP linearization move, with exact line search."""
    if linprog is None or sparse is None or time_limit <= 30.0:
        return p, exact_score(p)

    p = sanitize_sequence(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0 or n > 1024:
        return p, exact_score(p)

    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.01:
        return p, exact_score(p)
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best_p)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit)

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for it in range(max(1, int(max_iter))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35.0:
            break

        try:
            q = fft_convolution(best_p)
            M = float(np.max(np.maximum(q, 0.0)))
        except Exception:
            break
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = build_lp_matrix(best_p)
        if A is None:
            break

        improved = False
        for factor in (1.0, 1.05, 0.95):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
                break
            b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M * factor), dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, 100.0)] * n
            b_ub = b_ub.astype(np.float64, copy=False)

            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c_obj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": max(5.0, time_limit - 60.0)},
                )
            except Exception:
                try:
                    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
                except Exception:
                    res = None

            if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
                continue

            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 100.0)
            gs = float(np.sum(g))
            if gs <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(gs):
                continue
            g = g / gs

            # Exact line search.
            ts = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00)
            local_best = None
            local_sc = best_score
            for t in ts:
                mixed = (1.0 - t) * best_p + t * g
                sc = exact_score(mixed)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc:
                    local_sc = sc
                    local_best = mixed.copy()
            if local_best is not None:
                best_p = local_best.copy()
                best_score = local_sc
                improved = True
                print(f"LP move it={it} factor={factor} t=? score={best_score:.10f}")
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    final = make_valid_sequence(best_p, fallback_n=n)
    final_sc = exact_score(final)
    if math.isfinite(final_sc) and final_sc < best_score:
        best_p, best_score = final, final_sc
    return best_p, best_score


def make_block_perturb(a, rng, sigma):
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    out = a.copy()

    nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    maxw = max(1, min(96, n // 8))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, maxw + 1)))
        width = max(1, min(n, width))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        scale = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), width))
        scale = np.nan_to_num(scale, nan=1.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=1.0)
        out[start:start + width] *= scale

    if rng.random() < 0.20:
        out = 0.5 * (out + out[::-1])

    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=UB_FINAL, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, UB_FINAL)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
        out = out / s
    return out


def anneal_fast(a, budget, rng):
    if budget <= 2.0:
        return a, exact_score(a)

    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a, exact_score(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    cur = a.copy()
    cur_score = fast_score(cur)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score(best)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget)
    sigma = 0.12
    T = 0.008
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step += 1
        cand = make_block_perturb(cur, rng, sigma)
        sc = fast_score(cand)
        accept = False
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            if sc <= cur_score:
                accept = True
            else:
                diff = sc - cur_score
                if diff < 12.0 * max(T, 1e-9):
                    pacc = math.exp(-diff / max(T, 1e-9))
                    accept = bool(rng.random() < pacc)

        if accept:
            cur = cand.copy()
            cur_score = sc
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 90:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = fast_score(cur)
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = max(0.02, 0.72 * sigma)
            T = max(1e-4, 0.72 * T)

        if step % 35 == 0:
            sigma = max(0.018, 0.985 * sigma)
            T = max(1e-5, 0.99 * T)

        if step % 500 == 0 and time.monotonic() - last_print > 2.0:
            print(f"anneal step={step} fast_best={best_score:.10f} sigma={sigma:.4f}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    # Authoritative exact check on the fastest candidate.
    cand_score = exact_score(best)
    if math.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score - 1e-12 < best_score:
        best_score = cand_score

    # Keep original if anneal did not improve by exact score.
    orig_score = exact_score(a)
    if math.isfinite(orig_score) and orig_score <= cand_score + 1e-12:
        if orig_score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = orig_score
    best = make_valid_sequence(best, fallback_n=n)
    final_sc = exact_score(best)
    if math.isfinite(final_sc) and final_sc < best_score:
        best_score = final_sc
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400):
    """
    Main search entry point.
    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 42
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + total_budget - 2.5

    # Robust baseline: several central sequences and any prior best global.
    best_a = central_seq(128).astype(np.float64)
    best_score = exact_score(best_a)
    print(f"initial central score={best_score:.10f}")

    for n in (64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
        a = central_seq(n).astype(np.float64)
        sc = exact_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if hs.size > 0:
            sc = exact_score(hs)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_a = hs.copy()
                best_score = sc
                print(f"prior height_sequence_1 score={best_score:.10f} n={hs.size}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining <= 0:
        return [float(v) for v in make_valid_sequence(best_a, 128)]

    # 1) Two-scale product constructions.
    if remaining > 40.0:
        product_budget = min(170.0, max(15.0, 0.48 * remaining - 25.0))
        product_factors = [
            (1024, 32, 32),
            (1024, 64, 16),
            (1024, 16, 64),
            (512, 32, 16),
            (512, 16, 32),
            (2048, 64, 32),
            (2048, 32, 64),
            (1024, 128, 8),
            (1024, 8, 128),
            (2048, 128, 16),
            (2048, 16, 128),
            (1024, 256, 4),
            (1024, 4, 256),
        ]
        best_a, best_score = optimize_product_factors(
            product_factors, best_a, best_score, product_budget, rng
        )

    # 2) Full softmax sequence optimization from promising candidates.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 35.0:
        cand_list = [
            best_a,
            central_seq(int(max(64, best_a.size))).astype(np.float64),
            make_valid_sequence(best_a, fallback_n=1024),
        ]
        try:
            cand_list.append(np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
        except Exception:
            pass
        # A quickly generated two-scale product candidate if best_a is not product-shaped.
        try:
            m = 32
            r = max(1, int(round(1024 / m)))
            st = make_product_state(m, r, "central", "central", rng)
            cand_list.append(product_state_to_p(st, m, r))
        except Exception:
            pass

        full_budget = min(120.0, max(12.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - 60.0))
        if full_budget > 20.0:
            best_a, best_score = optimize_full_set(
                cand_list, best_a, best_score, full_budget, rng
            )

    # 3) Sparse LP move for moderate lengths.
    if (
        linprog is not None
        and sparse is not None
        and best_a.size >= 2
        and best_a.size <= 1024
        and deadline - time.monotonic() > 70.0
        and best_score > TARGET_UPPER_BOUND - 1e-9
    ):
        lp_budget = min(90.0, max(35.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - 35.0))
        best_a, best_score = lp_move(best_a, lp_budget, max_iter=3)

    # 4) Final annealing / block hill-climber.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 12.0:
        if best_a.size <= 256:
            anneal_budget = min(max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 3.0), 70.0)
        elif best_a.size <= 1024:
            anneal_budget = min(max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 3.0), 55.0)
        else:
            anneal_budget = min(max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 3.0), 25.0)
        if anneal_budget > 3.0:
            best_a, best_score = anneal_fast(best_a, anneal_budget, rng)

    # Final validation and fallback.
    best_a = make_valid_sequence(best_a, fallback_n=128)
    final_score = exact_score(best_a)
    if math.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    try:
        ext = float(evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in best_a]))
        if math.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_score:
            best_score = ext
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_a = sanitize_sequence(best_a)
    if best_a.size == 0 or float(np.sum(best_a)) < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(exact_score(best_a)):
        best_a = central_seq(128).astype(np.float64)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)

    print(f"FINAL score={best_score:.10f} n={best_a.size}")
    return [float(v) for v in best_a]
```
